OverviewDivergencesRulingsCrashes

Divergences

41,860 tests where the newest rakupp observation on record disagrees with the reference, in 3,814 clusters by message shape. A cluster is usually one root cause; the big ones are the fix-drivers.

Each count reflects what stood when the cell was last probed — the engine build is named on every record. Adjudicated divergences carry a signed verdict and live under Rulings.
